<!--
 Interactive script to simulate engine initialization readiness -->
<script>
    
document.addEventListener
('
DOMContentLoaded
', () => {
      
const
 
statusText
 = 
document.querySelector
('footer p');
      
const
 messages = [
        'Initializing capture engine...',
        'Calibrating 120 FPS render buffer...',
        'Configuring lossless audio bus...',
        'Ready to record'
      ];
      
      let index = 0;
      
const
 interval = 
setInterval
(
() => {
        index++;
        if (index < 
messages.length
) {
          if (
statusText
) {
            
statusText.textContent
 = messages[index];
            if (index === 
messages.length
 - 1) {
              
const
 spinner = 
statusText.previousElementSibling
;
              if (spinner) {
                
spinner.textContent
 = '
check_circle
';
                
spinner.classList.remove
('animate-spin');
                
spinner.classList.add
('text-[#DC2626]');
              }
            }
          }
        } else {
          
clearInterval
(interval);
        }
      }, 1100);
    });
  </script>
</body></html>

<!--
 Client JavaScript for dynamic walkthrough, pagination and CTA evolution -->
<script>
    (
function(
) {
      let 
currentSlide
 = 0;
      
const
 
totalSlides
 = 3;
      
      
const
 track = 
document.getElementById
('
carouselTrack
');
      
const
 dots = 
document.querySelectorAll
('.dot-indicator');
      
const
 
nextBtn
 = 
document.getElementById
('
nextBtn
');
      
const
 
nextBtnLabel
 = 
document.getElementById
('
nextBtnLabel
');
      
const
 
nextBtnIcon
 = 
document.getElementById
('
nextBtnIcon
');
      
const
 
prevBtn
 = 
document.getElementById
('
prevBtn
');
      
const
 
skipBtn
 = 
document.getElementById
('
skipBtn
');
      function 
updateSlide
(index) {
        if (index < 0) index = 0;
        if (index >= 
totalSlides
) index = 
totalSlides
 - 1;
        
        
currentSlide
 = index;
        
        // Slide track transformation
        
track.style
.transform
 = `
translateX
(
-${
currentSlide
 * 
100}%
)`;
        // Update pagination indicators
        
dots.forEach
((dot, 
idx
) => {
          if (
idx
 === 
currentSlide
) {
            
dot.className
 = "dot-indicator h-2 w-7 rounded-full 
bg
-[#DC2626] shadow-
sm
 transition-all duration-300";
          } else {
            
dot.className
 = "dot-indicator h-2 w-2 rounded-full 
bg
-[#D1D5DB] hover:bg-gray-400 transition-all duration-300";
          }
        });
        // Update Prev Button visibility
        if (
currentSlide
 === 0) {
          
prevBtn.classList.add
(
'opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        } else {
          
prevBtn.classList.remove
('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        }
        // Update Next Button State & Label (Transform to 'Get Started')
        if (
currentSlide
 === 
totalSlides
 - 1) {
          
nextBtnLabel.textContent
 = "Get Started";
          
nextBtnIcon.textContent
 = "
rocket_launch
";
          
nextBtn.classList.add
("recording-glow-pulse");
          
skipBtn.classList.add
(
"opacity-0", "pointer-events-none");
        } else {
          
nextBtnLabel.textContent
 = "Next";
          
nextBtnIcon.textContent
 = "
arrow_forward
";
          
nextBtn.classList.remove
("recording-glow-pulse");
          
skipBtn.classList.remove
("opacity-0", "pointer-events-none");
        }
      }
      // Next / CTA Action
      
nextBtn.addEventListener
('click', () => {
        if (
currentSlide
 < 
totalSlides
 - 1) {
          
updateSlide
(
currentSlide
 + 1);
        } else {
          // Final Slide Get Started Action
          
nextBtn.classList.add
('scale-95');
          
setTimeout
(
() => {
            
alert(
'Welcome to Consolidated Screen Recorder Studio! Permissions requested.');
          }, 150);
        }
      });
      // Previous Action
      
prevBtn.addEventListener
('click', () => {
        if (
currentSlide
 > 0) {
          
updateSlide
(
currentSlide
 - 1);
        }
      });
      // Skip Button
      
skipBtn.addEventListener
('click', () => {
        
updateSlide
(
totalSlides
 - 1);
      });
      // Direct Pagination Click
      
dots.forEach
(dot => {
        
dot.addEventListener
('click', (e) => {
          
const
 
targetIndex
 = 
parseInt
(
e.currentTarget.getAttribute
('data-index'), 10);
          
updateSlide
(
targetIndex
);
        });
      });
      // Touch / Swipe Navigation Support for Mobile
      let 
touchStartX
 = 0;
      let 
touchEndX
 = 0;
      
track.addEventListener
('
touchstart
', (e) => {
        
touchStartX
 = 
e.changedTouches
[0
].
screenX
;
      }, 
{ passive
: 
true }
);
      
track.addEventListener
('
touchend
', (e) => {
        
touchEndX
 = 
e.changedTouches
[0
].
screenX
;
        
handleSwipe
(
);
      }, 
{ passive
: 
true }
);
      function 
handleSwipe
(
) {
        
const
 threshold = 45;
        if (
touchEndX
 < 
touchStartX
 - threshold) {
          // Swipe left -> Next
          if (
currentSlide
 < 
totalSlides
 - 1) {
            
updateSlide
(
currentSlide
 + 1);
          }
        }
        if (
touchEndX
 > 
touchStartX
 + threshold) {
          // Swipe right -> Prev
          if (
currentSlide
 > 0) {
            
updateSlide
(
currentSlide
 - 1);
          }
        }
      }
    })();
  </script>

<!--
 Lightweight Interaction Script for Mode Switching -->
<script>
    
document.addEventListener
('
DOMContentLoaded
', () => {
      
const
 cards = 
document.querySelectorAll
(
'.mode
-card');
      
cards.forEach
(card => {
        
card.addEventListener
('click', () => {
          // Reset all cards
          
cards.forEach
(c => {
            
c.classList.remove
('border-2', 'border-red-500', 'recording-radial-glow');
            
c.classList.add
(
'border', 'border-gray-200');
            
            // Remove radial glow child if exists
            
const
 glow = 
c.querySelector
(
'.blur
-2xl');
            if (glow) 
glow.remove
();
            // Reset icon container
            
const
 
iconBox
 = 
c.querySelector
(
'.w
-11');
            if (
iconBox
) {
              
iconBox.className
 = 'w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-gray-50 flex items-
center
 justify-
center
 border border-gray-200 text-gray-700 group-hover:text-gray-900 group-hover:bg-gray-100 transition-
colors
';
              
const
 icon = 
iconBox.querySelector
(
'.material
-symbols-outlined');
              if (icon) 
icon.classList.remove
('material-symbols-fill');
            }
            // Reset radio pip indicator
            
const
 pip = 
c.querySelector
(
'.w
-6');
            if (pip) {
              
pip.className
 = 'w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-gray-300 flex items-
center
 justify-
center
 
bg
-transparent';
              
pip.innerHTML
 = '<div class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full 
bg
-transparent"></div>';
            }
          });
          // Activate clicked card
          
card.classList.remove
('border', 'border-gray-200');
          
card.classList.add
(
'border-2', 'border-red-500', 'recording-radial-glow');
          // Inject subtle ambient backlight accent
          
const
 glow = 
document.createElement
('div');
          
glow.className
 = 'absolute -top-12 -right-12 w-32 h-32 bg-red-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none';
          
card.prepend
(glow);
          // Update icon container
          
const
 
iconBox
 = 
card.querySelector
(
'.w
-11');
          if (
iconBox
) {
            
iconBox.className
 = 'w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-red-50 flex items-
center
 justify-
center
 border border-red-200 text-red-600 group-
hover:border
-red-400 transition-
colors
 shadow-
sm
';
            
const
 icon = 
iconBox.querySelector
(
'.material
-symbols-outlined');
            if (icon) 
icon.classList.add
('material-symbols-fill');
          }
          // Radio checked indicator
          
const
 pip = 
card.querySelector
(
'.w
-6');
          if (pip) {
            
pip.className
 = 'w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-red-600 flex items-
center
 justify-
center
 text-white shadow-
sm
 ring-2 ring-red-100';
            
pip.innerHTML
 = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[15px] font-bold" data-icon="check">check</span>';
          }
          
const
 
radioInput
 = 
card.querySelector
('input[type="radio"]');
          if (
radioInput
) 
radioInput.checked
 = true;
        });
      });
    });
  </script>

    // Lightweight micro-interaction for Bitrate Slider
    
const
 
bitrateSlider
 = 
document.getElementById
('bitrate-slider');
    
const
 
bitrateDisplay
 = 
document.getElementById
('bitrate-display');
    if (

 &&

) {

('input', (e) => {

 = `${
e.target
.value
} Mbps`;
      });
    }
    // Toggle switch interaction
    
const
 
toggleSwitches
 = 
document.querySelectorAll
('button[role="switch"]');
    
toggleSwitches.forEach
((
btn
) => {
      
btn.addEventListener
('click', () => {
        
const
 
isChecked
 = 
btn.getAttribute
('aria-checked') === 'true';
        
btn.setAttribute
('aria-checked', 
(!
isChecked
).
toString
());
        if 
(!
isChecked
) {
          
btn.classList.remove
('bg-gray-200', 'justify-start');
          
btn.classList.add
(
'bg-red-600', 'justify-end', 'shadow-inner');
        } else {
          
btn.classList.remove
('bg-red-600', 'justify-end', 'shadow-inner');
          
btn.classList.add
(
'bg-gray-200', 'justify-start');
        }
      });
    });
  </script>

<!--
 Script for realistic countdown pulse & dismiss interaction -->
<script>
    
document.addEventListener
('
DOMContentLoaded
', () => {
      
const
 
numElem
 = 
document.getElementById
('countdown-number');
      
const
 
cancelBtn
 = 
document.getElementById
('cancel-
btn
');
      let 
currentVal
 = 3;
      
const
 
countdownInterval
 = 
setInterval
(
() => {
        if (
currentVal
 > 1) {
          
currentVal
--;
          
numElem.classList.add
(
'scale-75', 'opacity-50');
          
setTimeout
(
() => {
            
numElem.textContent
 = 
currentVal
;
            
numElem.classList.remove
('scale-75', 'opacity-50');
          }, 120);
        } else {
          
clearInterval
(
countdownInterval
);
          
numElem.classList.add
(
'scale-75', 'opacity-50');
          
setTimeout
(
() => {
            
numElem.textContent
 = 'REC';
            
numElem.classList.add
('text-primary-container');
            
numElem.classList.remove
('scale-75', 'opacity-50');
          }, 120);
        }
      }, 1000);
      
cancelBtn.addEventListener
('click', (e) => {
        
e.stopPropagation
();
        
clearInterval
(
countdownInterval
);
        
numElem.textContent
 = 'STOP';
        
numElem.classList.add
('text-secondary');
      });
    });
  </script>

 Interactive JavaScript Logic -->
<script>
    // Live Recording Timer Simulation
    let 
secondsElapsed
 = 755; // 00:12:35
    let 
isPaused
 = false;
    
const
 
timerElem
 = 
document.getElementById
('
recordTimer
');
    
const
 
pauseToggleBtn
 = 
document.getElementById
('
pauseToggleBtn
');
    
const
 
pauseIcon
 = 
document.getElementById
('
pauseIcon
');
    
setInterval
(
() => {
      if 
(!
isPaused
) {
        
secondsElapsed
++;
        
const
 hrs = 
String(
Math.floor
(
secondsElapsed
 / 3600)
).
padStart
(2, '0');
        
const
 mins = 
String(
Math.floor
((
secondsElapsed
 % 3600) / 60)
).
padStart
(2, '0');
        
const
 secs = 
String(
secondsElapsed
 % 60
).
padStart
(2, '0');
        
timerElem.textContent
 = `${hrs
}:$
{mins
}:$
{secs}`;
      }
    }, 1000);
    // Pause / Resume Toggle Action
    
pauseToggleBtn.addEventListener
('click', () => {
      
isPaused
 
= !
isPaused
;
      if (
isPaused
) {
        
pauseIcon.textContent
 = '
play_arrow
';
        
pauseToggleBtn.classList.add
('text-primary-container');
        
timerElem.classList.add
('opacity-40');
      } else {
        
pauseIcon.textContent
 = 'pause';
        
pauseToggleBtn.classList.remove
('text-primary-container');
        
timerElem.classList.remove
('opacity-40');
      }
    });
    // Mic Toggle Action
    
const
 
micToggleBtn
 = 
document.getElementById
('
micToggleBtn
');
    
const
 
micIcon
 = 
document.getElementById
('
micIcon
');
    
const
 
micPip
 = 
document.getElementById
('
micPip
');
    let 
micActive
 = true;
    
micToggleBtn.addEventListener
('click', () => {
      
micActive
 
= !
micActive
;
      if (
micActive
) {
        
micIcon.textContent
 = 'mic';
        
micToggleBtn.classList.add
(
'
bg
-secondary-container', 'text-primary-container');
        
micToggleBtn.classList.remove
('
bg
-surface-container-high/80', 'text-on-surface-variant');
        
micPip.classList.remove
('hidden');
      } else {
        
micIcon.textContent
 = '
mic_off
';
        
micToggleBtn.classList.remove
('
bg
-secondary-container', 'text-primary-container');
        
micToggleBtn.classList.add
(
'
bg
-surface-container-high/80', 'text-on-surface-variant');
        
micPip.classList.add
('hidden');
      }
    });
    // Stop Recording Feedback Interaction
    
const
 
stopRecordBtn
 = 
document.getElementById
('
stopRecordBtn
');
    
stopRecordBtn.addEventListener
('click', () => {
      
isPaused
 = true;
      
const
 
originalText
 = 
timerElem.textContent
;
      
timerElem.textContent
 = "SAVED";
      
timerElem.classList.add
('text-primary');
      
setTimeout
(
() => {
        
timerElem.textContent
 = 
originalText
;
        
timerElem.classList.remove
('text-primary');
        
isPaused
 = false;
      }, 1500);
    });
  </script>
</body></html>

 Interactive State Micro-Interactions -->
<script>
    
document.addEventListener
('
DOMContentLoaded
', () => {
      
const
 
playPauseBtn
 = 
document.getElementById
('
playPauseBtn
');
      
const
 
playIcon
 = 
document.getElementById
('
playIcon
');
      let 
isPlaying
 = true;
      
playPauseBtn.addEventListener
('click', () => {
        
isPlaying
 
= !
isPlaying
;
        if (
isPlaying
) {
          
playIcon.textContent
 = 'pause';
          
playPauseBtn.classList.add
('custom-glow');
        } else {
          
playIcon.textContent
 = '
play_arrow
';
          
playPauseBtn.classList.remove
('custom-glow');
        }
      });
      // Playback speed selector interaction
      
const
 
speedButtons
 = 
document.querySelectorAll
('#
speedControls
 button');
      
speedButtons.forEach
(
btn
 => {
        
btn.addEventListener
('click', () => {
          
speedButtons.forEach
(b => {
            
b.className
 = 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-label-
sm
 font-label-
sm
 font-semibold bg-gray-100 border border-gray-200 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 
active:scale
-95 transition-all';
          });
          
btn.className
 = 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-label-
sm
 font-label-
sm
 font-semibold 
bg
-[#dc2626] text-white border border-[#dc2626] 
active:scale
-95 transition-all shadow-
sm
';
        });
      });
    });
  </script>
